# Football Total-Goals Model: Version 1 Report

_First run, made with the prototype scripts before this package existed. The prediction log was seeded from this run. Later runs come from `python -m fpredict run`._

Data timestamp: stats and odds retrieved 2026-11-08 20:53 UTC on 8 Nov 2026. Stats source: PitchAPI (2026/2027 season). Odds source: The Odds API (UK region, `totals` + `alternate_totals`).

## ANALYSIS SUMMARY

- Fixtures in the Odds API feed for the 10 eligible leagues: 70
- Excluded: 6 matches that had already kicked off on 8 Nov (Lens v Marseille, Fiorentina v Juventus, Utrecht v NEC, Estrela v Benfica, Rio Ave v Braga, Atlético Madrid v Barcelona). Belgian Pro League and Turkish Super Lig: the Odds API returned no fixtures (league not active in the feed). Portuguese Primeira Liga: no fixtures after tonight in the feed. Note: Rayo Vallecano's sample spans 19 Sep–8 Nov because their 10 Oct match v Athletic Club is listed as not played (postponed).
- Eligible and not started: 64
- Matches modelled: 64 (all have five completed current-season league matches per team, with no missing xG, shots, SOT or corners values)
- Model type: Preferred xG model for all 64 matches. No fallback model was used.
- Matches with at least one goal line priced by a UK sportsbook: 60. Matches with no sportsbook price on any of the three lines: 4
- Selections with model edge ≥ +4.0pp (one per match): 12
- Caps applied: 32 (RC Lens v Toulouse: Away def. weakness 0.560→0.65; Feyenoord v Excelsior: Away attack 0.391→0.65; Barcelona v Villarreal: Home attack 1.573→1.50; Barcelona v Villarreal: Away def. weakness 0.537→0.65; Hull City v Brighton and Hove Albion: Home attack 0.597→0.65; AC Milan v Frosinone: Away attack 1.634→1.50; Rangers v Aberdeen: Home attack 1.578→1.50; FC Schalke 04 v VfB Stuttgart: Home attack 0.647→0.65; Newcastle United v Arsenal: Away def. weakness 0.575→0.65; Sassuolo v Genoa: Home attack 0.452→0.65; Sassuolo v Genoa: Home def. weakness 1.686→1.50; Getafe v Atlético Madrid: Home attack 0.582→0.65; Real Madrid v Celta Vigo: Home attack 1.524→1.50; Real Madrid v Celta Vigo: Home def. weakness 0.620→0.65; Coventry City v Crystal Palace: Away def. weakness 1.688→1.50; Brentford v Everton: Home attack 0.619→0.65; Juventus v Venezia: Home def. weakness 0.568→0.65; Motherwell v Rangers: Away attack 1.578→1.50; Union Berlin v RB Leipzig: Home attack 0.398→0.65; Bayern Munich v 1. ## MODEL INPUTS AND AUDIT TRAIL

Weights: recency 30/25/20/15/10 (most recent first). Attack = 0.50·xG + 0.25·SOT + 0.15·Shots + 0.10·Corners ratios; Defensive Weakness uses the conceded equivalents. Scores capped 0.65–1.50. Final xG = 0.75·raw + 0.25·league average. Poisson λ = expected total goals. Version 1 coefficients are provisional and have not been backtested.
